<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      transition.finished.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(done, done)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    [duration, reduceMotion]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  )</span></span>
